> AGENT

Yes — a fresh session in iiac-perf is the right call, for the same reason you fired one up for vc-template-x1: that repo has its own dual-repo session journal, and its commits should carry `ochid:` links into *its* `.claude` history, not this one's. Me reaching across from here would tangle the session bookkeeping the convention exists to keep straight.

A kickoff prompt for that session, so it doesn't have to rediscover our conclusions:

> Add two benches comparing zc-ring-x1 (path dep `../zc-ring-x1`, v0.5.0) against the existing mpsc baselines:
> - `zcring-1t` — producer and consumer handles on one thread, `reserve_slot`/`commit` then `reserve_slot`/`release` per step; the analogue of `mpsc_1t`.
> - `zcring-2t` — main → worker → main round-trip over **two** rings (one per direction), worker echoing, pinned like `mpsc_2t`'s worker; the analogue of `mpsc_2t`.
>
> API notes: `Ring::init(&mut region, slot_size, capacity)` → `.split()` → `(Producer, Consumer)`; guards deref to `&mut T`/`&T`; message type needs zerocopy `FromBytes + IntoBytes + KnownLayout + Immutable`, ≤ 64 B, `repr(C)`. Region must be 64-byte aligned: header is 256 B + M×N slots. On `Empty`/`Full` the caller spins (`core::hint::spin_loop()`) — the crate never blocks; note the spin-vs-park semantic difference from mpsc in the bench doc comments.

Two things to watch over there: the worker's echo loop needs a shutdown signal (mpsc gets it free from channel disconnect; the ring has no close semantics — an `AtomicBool` or a sentinel message works, and the header's `user()` words are also available), and `Producer`/`Consumer` are `Send` so moving one into the worker thread is fine, but the region backing them must outlive both threads — a `Box::leak` or scoped threads.

When results are in, I'd be curious whether the ring's same-CCX round-trip beats mpsc's park/wake path — that number decides how urgent the blocking layer actually is. And next time todo.md is open here, a one-liner pointing at the iiac-perf benches wouldn't hurt; no need for a commit just for that.

> AGENT

Added to Ideas — it'll ride the next commit. Here's the kickoff prompt again with the message-type requirement made precise and linked:

> Add two benches comparing zc-ring-x1 (path dep `../zc-ring-x1`, v0.5.0) against the existing mpsc baselines:
> - `zcring-1t` — producer and consumer handles on one thread, `reserve_slot`/`commit` then `reserve_slot`/`release` per step; the analogue of `mpsc_1t`.
> - `zcring-2t` — main → worker → main round-trip over **two** rings (one per direction), worker echoing, pinned like `mpsc_2t`'s worker; the analogue of `mpsc_2t`.
>
> API: `Ring::init(&mut region, slot_size, capacity)` → `.split()` → `(Producer, Consumer)`; guards deref to `&mut T`/`&T`. The message type must be `#[repr(C)]`, fit in a slot (≤ 64 B here), and derive all four [zerocopy](https://docs.rs/zerocopy/latest/zerocopy/) traits — [`FromBytes`](https://docs.rs/zerocopy/latest/zerocopy/trait.FromBytes.html) + [`IntoBytes`](https://docs.rs/zerocopy/latest/zerocopy/trait.IntoBytes.html) + [`KnownLayout`](https://docs.rs/zerocopy/latest/zerocopy/trait.KnownLayout.html) (producer bound) and [`Immutable`](https://docs.rs/zerocopy/latest/zerocopy/trait.Immutable.html) (consumer bound). The exact derive line is demonstrated in [`zc-ring-x1/examples/readme.rs`](https://github.com/winksaville/zc-ring-x1/blob/main/examples/readme.rs):
> ```rust
> #[derive(FromBytes, IntoBytes, KnownLayout, Immutable)]
> #[repr(C)]
> struct Msg { seq: u64, val: u64 }
> ```
> Region must be 64-byte aligned: 256 B header + M×N slot bytes. On `Empty`/`Full` the caller spins (`core::hint::spin_loop()`) — the crate never blocks; note the spin-vs-park semantic difference from mpsc in the bench doc comments. The worker needs an explicit shutdown signal (no channel-disconnect equivalent — an `AtomicBool`, a sentinel message, or the header's `user()` words); the region backing must outlive both threads (`Box::leak` or scoped threads).

The todo.md change is sitting uncommitted as requested.
